- **font-optimization-checker**: You are the font optimization checker specialist for GSMFWs. ([engremran07/gsmvault](https://github.com/engremran07/gsmvault/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/font-optimization-checker.md))
- **font-outline-reviewer**: Independent visual review of glyph render sheets made by the font-outline-repair skill. Use after a repair round to judge, glyph by glyph and weight by weight, whether outline problems are gone and whether the fixes created new ones. Reads PNGs and audit JSON; never edits fonts. ([FreekMencke/claude-skills](https://github.com/FreekMencke/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/freek-local-skills/agents/font-outline-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **font-researcher-display-feral**: Researches DISPLAY / SCRIPT / WEIRD fonts from user-submitted repositories (1001fonts, DaFont, FontSpace). Returns 2–4 candidates as YAML. Dispatched by font-hunt skill. Hunts the 10% of personality-rich fonts in a sea of 90% trash. ([likeahuman-ai/font-hunt](https://github.com/likeahuman-ai/font-hunt/blob/HEAD/agents/font-researcher-display-feral.md))
- **font-researcher-experimental**: Researches EXPERIMENTAL, open-source, rule-breaking fonts from Velvetyne, Future Fonts, Use & Modify, Collletttivo. Returns 2–4 candidates matching a brief, as YAML. Dispatched by the font-hunt skill. Do not invoke directly unless replicating its dispatch protocol. ([likeahuman-ai/font-hunt](https://github.com/likeahuman-ai/font-hunt/blob/HEAD/agents/font-researcher-experimental.md))
- **font-researcher-free-curated**: Researches high-craft FREE fonts from curated foundries (Fontshare, Pangram Pangram, League of Moveable Type, Fontsource, Uncut.wtf, Open Foundry). Returns 2–4 candidates matching a brief, as YAML. Dispatched by the font-hunt skill. Do not invoke directly unless replicating its dispatch protocol. ([likeahuman-ai/font-hunt](https://github.com/likeahuman-ai/font-hunt/blob/HEAD/agents/font-researcher-free-curated.md))
- **font-researcher-google-adobe**: Researches the UNDERUSED GEMS from Google Fonts and Adobe Fonts. Absolutely refuses to return Inter, Montserrat, Poppins, Roboto, Playfair Display, DM Sans, Satoshi, Clash Display, etc. Returns 2–4 candidates as YAML. Dispatched by the font-hunt skill. ([likeahuman-ai/font-hunt](https://github.com/likeahuman-ai/font-hunt/blob/HEAD/agents/font-researcher-google-adobe.md))
- **font-researcher-paid-indie**: Researches PAID fonts from top independent foundries (Klim, Grilli Type, Dinamo, OH no Type, Sharp Type, Colophon, Commercial Type, Production Type, Displaay). Returns 2–4 candidates as YAML. Dispatched by the font-hunt skill. Users are told these are paid — surfaces the "if budget allows" tier. ([likeahuman-ai/font-hunt](https://github.com/likeahuman-ai/font-hunt/blob/HEAD/agents/font-researcher-paid-indie.md))
- **font-runner**: Runs Myia Didot's mechanical build loop and reports only the outcome. Use it for regenerating the generated styles (make generate), running the test suite (make test or a pytest selection), checking that the generated styles are reproducible (make reproduce), building fonts (make otf) or proofs (make proof), and updating test baselines when told exactly which ones and why. It does not decide anything about the design and does not commit. ([ghschaden/MyiaDidot](https://github.com/ghschaden/MyiaDidot/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/font-runner.md))
- **font-selector**: Selects and HarfBuzz-verifies a ghost font for jayasree's language-onboarding pipeline (docs/LANGUAGE_ONBOARDING_AGENTS.md's Agent 2). Use only when explicitly asked to find/verify a font for a language profile that already has human sign-off. - **fontawesome-icon-bundler**: 1. **Fonts corrupted?** Ruled out — built a standalone Playwright/HTML test (`@font-face` against the actual bundled `.ttf` files) and confirmed all icon glyphs render correctly in a browser. 2. **`fonts.AddFont` alias not resolving on unpackaged Windows?** Tried bypassing it with the documented raw `"&lt;file&gt;.ttf#&lt;internal family name&gt;"` WinUI `FontFamily` string (both relative and, later, absolute ([alaamer12/MostaqlK](https://github.com/alaamer12/MostaqlK/blob/HEAD/.repertoire/agents/fontawesome-icon-bundler.md))
- **fonte-implementer**: Implementa UMA story do monorepo fonte de ponta a ponta — lê o plano, codifica seguindo os padrões do repo, roda os gates (test:api + e2e, builds de contrato, cobertura ≥90 do escopo), atualiza o Postman, commita numa branch e devolve diff + hashes + bloqueios. É o motor de execução do AUTORUN (um spawn por story). Use para "implementar a story NN", "executar a unidade X" ou quando o orquestrador delega a codificação de uma fatia fechada. NÃO faz push, NÃO mergeia na main, NÃO pergunta nada ao usuário. ([visb/fonte](https://github.com/visb/fonte/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fonte-implementer.md))
- **fonte-scout**: Descobre fontes e identificadores para o acervo do Almanaque dos Clubes — deriva QIDs de artigos da Wikipédia, valida as entidades no Wikidata e inspeciona a estrutura real das tabelas (cabeçalhos, campeão/vice, artilheiro). Use ANTES de escrever qualquer seed/parser: devolve um relatório enxuto com os QIDs validados, os títulos de artigo que existem e os campeões esperados, sem tocar no banco. - **fonte-validator**: Roda os gates de validação do monorepo fonte (builds de contrato se packages/types ou api-client mudaram, migration:run:test se criou migration, test:api + test:api:e2e, runners de app tocados, cobertura ≥90 do escopo) e devolve PASS/FAIL com os erros exatos, comprimidos. Read-only — NÃO corrige nada, NÃO commita. Use para "valida essa mudança", "roda os gates", "confirma verde antes do merge", ou como bateria final delegada pelo orquestrador/implementer. ([visb/fonte](https://github.com/visb/fonte/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fonte-validator.md))

- **foresight**: Foresight Agent (Magenta) of the AITS system. Activate when multiple options exist and the future is uncertain — produces the options-scenarios matrix that ranks options by robustness across plausible futures. Triggered automatically by inviolable rule #5 when Green generates ≥ 4 viable options. Distinct from Predictive — Predictive simulates futures for one option; Foresight compares many options across many futures. Arbitrates Green-Black conflicts when options are being killed prematurely. ([fabiolalli/aits-agents](https://github.com/fabiolalli/aits-agents/blob/HEAD/spec/agents/foresight.md) · ★14)

- **foresight-architect**: Use to turn validated coverage gaps into ready-to-run regression proposals (in iterative-tdd's format) and to refine the priority/feature/serial reorg plan for hindsight. Writes proposals under .tdd/foresight/proposals/&lt;slug&gt;/ and reorg corrections to reorg/overrides.json; never writes into .tdd/regression/. Does not implement code or run the TDD loop — proposals are handed to /tdd for that.
